# 결함 111 ~ 113 (재검수 64 대응)

```text
111  grant_from_stored.rs — get_manifest_binding 오류 중 LegacyManifestMissing 만 Refused, 나머지 JobStoreError 는 Storage
112  From<String> for StoredGrantError 삭제 · 거부 지점마다 Refused
113  뮤테이션 원본에 패치 · 실패 출력
테스트  a_legacy_job_without_a_manifest_is_refused_and_the_listener_keeps_accepting(Manifest 행 삭제로 옛 Job 을 만든다)
뮤테이션 M42(다시 Storage) caught · M43(감싸지 않은 문자열) compile_error
```

물을 것:
1. get_manifest_binding 이 내는 다른 JobStoreError 중 **거부**여야 하는 것(상대 · 운영 사실)이 Storage 로 남았나, 반대로 Refused 로 간 저장소 장애가 있나.
2. 112 뒤에도 문자열이 조용히 Refused 가 되는 통로(다른 From · `?` 변환 · CLI 경로)가 남았나.
3. 111 테스트가 다른 이유로 통과할 수 있나 — 행 삭제가 기존 API 로 만든 옛 Job 과 다른 상태인가. 두 번째 연결을 받는 것이 리스너 유지의 충분한 증거인가
   (첫 연결 닫힘 대기 · connect_timeout 경쟁).
4. 원본의 M42 · M43 기록이 실패 원인을 입증하는가(113).

+/// 결함 111 (재검수 64) — Manifest 행이 없는 옛 hash-only Job 은 저장소 장애가 아니라 **거부**다. 첫 연결이 거부돼도 리스너는 두 번째
+///   연결을 받는다. 104 는 이것까지 Storage 로 묶어 옛 Job 하나로 리스너를 멈췄다.
+#[test]
+fn a_legacy_job_without_a_manifest_is_refused_and_the_listener_keeps_accepting() {
+    let fixture = fixture();
+    {
+        let db = rusqlite::Connection::open(&fixture.control_db).expect("control DB");
+        let changed = db
+            .execute("DELETE FROM coordinator_job_manifests WHERE job_id = ?1", [JOB_ID])
+            .expect("Manifest 행 삭제");
+        assert_eq!(changed, 1, "fixture 의 Manifest 행이 하나 있어야 한다");
+    }
+    let handle = spawn_coordinator_with(&fixture, 2, &[]);
+    let mut first = connect_when_ready(fixture.address);
+    send_hello(&mut first, gputeer_protocol::constants::MODE_MULTI_AGENT_GRANT, 0, 100);
+    // Coordinator 가 거부하고 닫을 때까지 기다린다. 닫힘은 EOF 로도 연결 재설정 오류로도 온다 — 어느 쪽이든 닫힌 것이다.
+    let mut rest = Vec::new();
+    let closed = first.read_to_end(&mut rest);
+    assert!(rest.is_empty(), "거부된 연결에 프레임이 왔다({} 바이트, {closed:?})", rest.len());
+    // Storage 로 분류했다면 리스너가 멈춰 이 연결이 거부되거나, 아래 join 이 저장소 문구로 끝난다.
+    let mut second = TcpStream::connect_timeout(&fixture.address, Duration::from_secs(2))
+        .expect("첫 거부 뒤에도 리스너가 두 번째 연결을 받아야 한다");
+    second.set_write_timeout(Some(Duration::from_secs(10))).expect("쓰기 타임아웃");
+    send_hello(&mut second, gputeer_protocol::constants::MODE_MULTI_AGENT_GRANT, 1, 120);
+    let error = handle
+        .join()
+        .expect("Coordinator 스레드")
+        .expect_err("마지막 연결의 거부는 오류로 끝난다");
+    assert!(
+        error.contains("GRANT_REFUSED") && error.contains("옛 Job") && !error.contains("저장된 Manifest 를 읽지 못했다"),
+        "{error}"
+    );
+}

+/// 결함 111 (재검수 64) — Manifest 행이 없는 옛 hash-only Job 은 저장소 장애가 아니라 **거부**다. 첫 연결이 거부돼도 리스너는 두 번째
+///   연결을 받는다. 104 는 이것까지 Storage 로 묶어 옛 Job 하나로 리스너를 멈췄다.
+#[test]
+fn a_legacy_job_without_a_manifest_is_refused_and_the_listener_keeps_accepting() {
+    let fixture = fixture();
+    {
+        let db = rusqlite::Connection::open(&fixture.control_db).expect("control DB");
+        let changed = db
+            .execute("DELETE FROM coordinator_job_manifests WHERE job_id = ?1", [JOB_ID])
+            .expect("Manifest 행 삭제");
+        assert_eq!(changed, 1, "fixture 의 Manifest 행이 하나 있어야 한다");
+    }
+    let handle = spawn_coordinator_with(&fixture, 2, &[]);
+    let mut first = connect_when_ready(fixture.address);
+    send_hello(&mut first, gputeer_protocol::constants::MODE_MULTI_AGENT_GRANT, 0, 100);
+    // Coordinator 가 거부하고 닫을 때까지 기다린다. 닫힘은 EOF 로도 연결 재설정 오류로도 온다 — 어느 쪽이든 닫힌 것이다.
+    let mut rest = Vec::new();
+    let closed = first.read_to_end(&mut rest);
+    assert!(rest.is_empty(), "거부된 연결에 프레임이 왔다({} 바이트, {closed:?})", rest.len());
+    // Storage 로 분류했다면 리스너가 멈춰 이 연결이 거부되거나, 아래 join 이 저장소 문구로 끝난다.
+    let mut second = TcpStream::connect_timeout(&fixture.address, Duration::from_secs(2))
+        .expect("첫 거부 뒤에도 리스너가 두 번째 연결을 받아야 한다");
+    second.set_write_timeout(Some(Duration::from_secs(10))).expect("쓰기 타임아웃");
+    send_hello(&mut second, gputeer_protocol::constants::MODE_MULTI_AGENT_GRANT, 1, 120);
+    let error = handle
+        .join()
+        .expect("Coordinator 스레드")
+        .expect_err("마지막 연결의 거부는 오류로 끝난다");
+    assert!(
+        error.contains("GRANT_REFUSED") && error.contains("옛 Job") && !error.contains("저장된 Manifest 를 읽지 못했다"),
+        "{error}"
+    );
+}

178: #[derive(Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
179: pub enum JobStoreError {
180:     InvalidInput(&'static str),
181:     NotFound,
182:     IdempotencyConflict {
183:         stored_job_id: String,
184:         requested_job_id: String,
185:     },
186:     JobIdConflict {
187:         job_id: String,
188:     },
189:     InvalidTransition {
190:         from: JobState,
191:         to: JobState,
192:     },
193:     PlanConflict {
194:         stored_plan_id: String,
195:         requested_plan_id: String,
196:     },
197:     GuardNotMet(&'static str),
198:     ManifestIdentityMismatch(&'static str),
199:     ManifestHashMismatch,
200:     LegacyManifestMissing {
201:         job_id: String,
202:     },
203:     ManifestCorrupt {
204:         job_id: String,
205:         kind: ManifestCorruption,
206:     },
207:     ClockRollback {
208:         earlier_unix_ms: u64,
209:         later_unix_ms: u64,
210:     },
211:     CorruptData(String),
212:     Io(String),
213:     LockTimeout,
214:     InjectedFailure(&'static str),
215: }
216: 
217: impl std::fmt::Display for JobStoreError {
218:     fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
219:         match self {
220:             Self::InvalidInput(field) => write!(f, "invalid job store input: {field}"),
221:             Self::NotFound => write!(f, "job_id is not present in the durable store"),
222:             Self::IdempotencyConflict {
223:                 stored_job_id,
224:                 requested_job_id,
225:             } => write!(
226:                 f,
227:                 "idempotency key conflict: stored job={stored_job_id}, requested job={requested_job_id}"
228:             ),
229:             Self::JobIdConflict { job_id } => {
230:                 write!(f, "job_id was already submitted with another key: {job_id}")
231:             }
232:             Self::InvalidTransition { from, to } => {
233:                 write!(f, "invalid durable Job transition: {from:?} -> {to:?}")
234:             }
235:             Self::PlanConflict {
236:                 stored_plan_id,
237:                 requested_plan_id,
238:             } => write!(
239:                 f,
240:                 "queued Job plan conflict: stored={stored_plan_id}, requested={requested_plan_id}"
241:             ),
242:             Self::GuardNotMet(guard) => write!(f, "Job transition guard not met: {guard}"),
243:             Self::ManifestIdentityMismatch(field) => {
244:                 write!(f, "verified Manifest identity does not match accepted {field}")
245:             }
246:             Self::ManifestHashMismatch => {
247:                 write!(f, "supplied manifest_hash does not match the verified Manifest")
248:             }
249:             Self::LegacyManifestMissing { job_id } => write!(
250:                 f,
251:                 "job {job_id} predates durable Manifest bodies and cannot be consumed"
252:             ),
253:             Self::ManifestCorrupt { job_id, kind } => {
254:                 write!(f, "durable Manifest for job {job_id} is corrupt: {kind:?}")
255:             }
256:             Self::ClockRollback {
257:                 earlier_unix_ms,
258:                 later_unix_ms,
259:             } => write!(
260:                 f,
261:                 "clock moved backwards: event={earlier_unix_ms}, prior={later_unix_ms}"
262:             ),
263:             Self::CorruptData(message) => write!(f, "job store corruption: {message}"),
264:             Self::Io(message) => write!(f, "job store I/O error: {message}"),
265:             Self::LockTimeout => write!(f, "job store lock acquisition timed out"),
266:             Self::InjectedFailure(point) => write!(f, "injected job store failure: {point}"),
267:         }
268:     }
269: }
270: 
271: impl std::error::Error for JobStoreError {}
272: 
273: pub(crate) fn map_sql_error(error: SqlError) -> JobStoreError {
274:     match error {
275:         SqlError::SqliteFailure(code, _)
276:             if matches!(
277:                 code.code,
278:                 ErrorCode::DatabaseBusy | ErrorCode::DatabaseLocked
279:             ) =>
280:         {
281:             JobStoreError::LockTimeout
282:         }
283:         SqlError::SqliteFailure(code, _) => JobStoreError::Io(code.to_string()),
284:         other => JobStoreError::Io(other.to_string()),
285:     }
286: }

288: pub(crate) fn encode_u64(value: u64) -> Vec<u8> {
289:     value.to_be_bytes().to_vec()
290: }
291: 
292: fn decode_u64(bytes: &[u8], field: &str) -> Result<u64, JobStoreError> {
293:     let value: [u8; 8] = bytes
294:         .try_into()
295:         .map_err(|_| JobStoreError::CorruptData(format!("{field} must contain exactly 8 bytes")))?;
296:     Ok(u64::from_be_bytes(value))
297: }
298: 
299: fn decode_hash(bytes: Vec<u8>) -> Result<[u8; 32], JobStoreError> {
300:     bytes.try_into().map_err(|_| {
301:         JobStoreError::CorruptData("manifest_hash must contain exactly 32 bytes".to_string())
302:     })
303: }

306:     connection: Connection,
307: }

309: pub(crate) fn initialize_schema(connection: &mut Connection) -> Result<(), JobStoreError> {
310:     connection
311:         .execute_batch(
312:             r#"
313:             PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON;
314:             PRAGMA journal_mode = DELETE;
315:             PRAGMA synchronous = FULL;
316: 
317:             CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS coordinator_jobs (
318:                 job_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
319:                 submitter_device_id TEXT NOT NULL,
320:                 manifest_hash BLOB NOT NULL,
321:                 state TEXT NOT NULL,
322:                 submitted_at_unix_ms BLOB NOT NULL,
323:                 planning_at_unix_ms BLOB,
324:                 queued_at_unix_ms BLOB,
325:                 staging_at_unix_ms BLOB,
326:                 deadline_unix_ms BLOB,
327:                 max_queue_duration_ms BLOB,
328:                 plan_id TEXT,
329:                 queue_failure_kind TEXT,
330:                 queue_failure_detail TEXT,
331:                 failed_at_unix_ms BLOB,
332:                 revision BLOB NOT NULL
333:             );
334: 
335:             CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS job_submission_idempotency (
336:                 idempotency_key BLOB PRIMARY KEY,
337:                 job_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES coordinator_jobs(job_id)
338:             );
339: 
340:             CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS coordinator_job_manifests (
341:                 job_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY REFERENCES coordinator_jobs(job_id),
342:                 verified_signer_id TEXT NOT NULL,
343:                 manifest_body BLOB NOT NULL
344:             );
345:             "#,
346:         )
347:         .map_err(map_sql_error)?;
348: 
349:     let transaction = connection
350:         .transaction_with_behavior(TransactionBehavior::Immediate)
351:         .map_err(map_sql_error)?;
352:     let has_staging_column = {
353:         let mut statement = transaction
354:             .prepare("PRAGMA table_info(coordinator_jobs)")
355:             .map_err(map_sql_error)?;
356:         let rows = statement
357:             .query_map([], |row| row.get::<_, String>(1))
358:             .map_err(map_sql_error)?;
359:         let mut found = false;
360:         for row in rows {
361:             if row.map_err(map_sql_error)? == "staging_at_unix_ms" {
362:                 found = true;
363:                 break;
364:             }
365:         }
366:         found
367:     };
368:     if !has_staging_column {
369:         transaction
370:             .execute(
371:                 "ALTER TABLE coordinator_jobs ADD COLUMN staging_at_unix_ms BLOB",
372:                 [],
373:             )
374:             .map_err(map_sql_error)?;
375:     }
376:     transaction.commit().map_err(map_sql_error)
377: }

379: impl CoordinatorJobStore {
380:     /// Opens a file-backed SQLite control store. Callers must reject
381:     /// `!is_durable()` in production, as the existing Lease store does.
382:     pub fn open(path: impl AsRef<Path>) -> Result<Self, JobStoreError> {
383:         let mut connection = Connection::open(path).map_err(map_sql_error)?;
384:         connection
385:             .busy_timeout(BUSY_TIMEOUT)
386:             .map_err(map_sql_error)?;
387:         initialize_schema(&mut connection)?;
388:         Ok(Self { connection })
389:     }
390: 
391:     pub fn is_durable(&self) -> bool {
392:         match self.connection.path() {
393:             Some(path) => !path.is_empty() && path != ":memory:",
394:             None => false,
395:         }
396:     }
397: 
398:     pub fn get(&self, job_id: &str) -> Result<Option<StoredJob>, JobStoreError> {
399:         fetch_job(&self.connection, job_id)
400:     }
401: 
402:     /// Loads a durable Manifest binding without claiming that its signature is
403:     /// still valid. Existing hash-only Jobs fail closed with
404:     /// [`JobStoreError::LegacyManifestMissing`].
405:     pub fn get_manifest_binding(
406:         &self,
407:         job_id: &str,
408:     ) -> Result<Option<StoredManifestBinding>, JobStoreError> {
409:         let Some(job) = fetch_job(&self.connection, job_id)? else {
410:             return Ok(None);
411:         };
412:         fetch_manifest_binding(&self.connection, &job).map(Some)
413:     }
414: 
415:     /// Returns the durable queue in deterministic FIFO order. `job_id` is the
416:     /// tie-breaker when two jobs have the same queue timestamp.

430: 
431:     /// Atomically creates an accepted Job and binds its 16-byte control-plane
432:     /// idempotency key. A byte-identical logical retry returns the original
433:     /// record, including its original submit timestamp; key reuse with changed
434:     /// immutable input fails closed.
435:     pub fn submit_accepted(
436:         &mut self,
437:         submission: &AcceptedJobSubmission,
438:         submitted_at_unix_ms: u64,
439:     ) -> Result<SubmitResult, JobStoreError> {
440:         validate_submission(submission)?;
441:         let transaction = self
442:             .connection
443:             .transaction_with_behavior(TransactionBehavior::Immediate)
444:             .map_err(map_sql_error)?;
445: 
446:         let mapped_job_id = transaction
447:             .query_row(
448:                 "SELECT job_id FROM job_submission_idempotency WHERE idempotency_key = ?1",
449:                 rusqlite::params![submission.idempotency_key.as_slice()],
450:                 |row| row.get::<_, String>(0),
451:             )
452:             .optional()
453:             .map_err(map_sql_error)?;
454: 
455:         if let Some(stored_job_id) = mapped_job_id {
456:             let stored = fetch_job(&transaction, &stored_job_id)?.ok_or_else(|| {
457:                 JobStoreError::CorruptData(format!(
458:                     "idempotency key points to missing job: {stored_job_id}"
459:                 ))
460:             })?;
461:             if !stored.matches_submission(submission) {
462:                 return Err(JobStoreError::IdempotencyConflict {
463:                     stored_job_id,
464:                     requested_job_id: submission.job_id.clone(),
465:                 });
466:             }
467:             transaction.commit().map_err(map_sql_error)?;
468:             return Ok(SubmitResult {
469:                 job: stored,
470:                 created: false,
471:             });
472:         }
473: 
474:         if fetch_job(&transaction, &submission.job_id)?.is_some() {
475:             return Err(JobStoreError::JobIdConflict {
476:                 job_id: submission.job_id.clone(),
477:             });
478:         }
479: 
480:         let stored = StoredJob {
481:             job_id: submission.job_id.clone(),
482:             submitter_device_id: submission.submitter_device_id.clone(),
483:             manifest_hash: submission.manifest_hash,
484:             state: JobState::Submitted,
485:             submitted_at_unix_ms,
486:             planning_at_unix_ms: None,
487:             queued_at_unix_ms: None,
488:             staging_at_unix_ms: None,
489:             deadline_unix_ms: submission.deadline_unix_ms,
490:             max_queue_duration_ms: submission.max_queue_duration_ms,
491:             plan_id: None,
492:             queue_failure: None,
493:             failed_at_unix_ms: None,
494:             revision: 0,
495:         };
496: 
497:         transaction
498:             .execute(
499:                 "INSERT INTO coordinator_jobs(
500:                     job_id, submitter_device_id, manifest_hash, state,
501:                     submitted_at_unix_ms, planning_at_unix_ms, queued_at_unix_ms, staging_at_unix_ms,
502:                     deadline_unix_ms, max_queue_duration_ms, plan_id,
503:                     queue_failure_kind, queue_failure_detail, failed_at_unix_ms, revision
504:                  ) VALUES (?1, ?2, ?3, ?4, ?5, NULL, NULL, NULL, ?6, ?7, NULL, NULL, NULL, NULL, ?8)",
505:                 rusqlite::params![
506:                     stored.job_id,
507:                     stored.submitter_device_id,
508:                     stored.manifest_hash.as_slice(),
509:                     stored.state.as_str(),
510:                     encode_u64(stored.submitted_at_unix_ms),
511:                     stored.deadline_unix_ms.map(encode_u64),
512:                     stored.max_queue_duration_ms.map(encode_u64),
513:                     encode_u64(stored.revision),
514:                 ],
515:             )
516:             .map_err(map_sql_error)?;
517:         transaction
518:             .execute(
519:                 "INSERT INTO job_submission_idempotency(idempotency_key, job_id) VALUES (?1, ?2)",
520:                 rusqlite::params![submission.idempotency_key.as_slice(), stored.job_id],
521:             )
522:             .map_err(map_sql_error)?;
523:         transaction.commit().map_err(map_sql_error)?;
524:         Ok(SubmitResult {
525:             job: stored,
526:             created: true,
527:         })
528:     }

530:     /// Atomically persists an accepted Job, its idempotency binding, and the
531:     /// complete signed Manifest. The type gate ensures that this storage path
532:     /// cannot receive an unverified Manifest.
533:     pub fn submit_verified_manifest(
534:         &mut self,
535:         submission: &AcceptedJobSubmission,
536:         manifest: &Verified<pb::JobManifest>,
537:         submitted_at_unix_ms: u64,
538:     ) -> Result<ManifestBoundSubmitResult, JobStoreError> {
539:         self.submit_verified_manifest_inner(submission, manifest, submitted_at_unix_ms, None)
540:     }
541: 
542:     fn submit_verified_manifest_inner(
543:         &mut self,
544:         submission: &AcceptedJobSubmission,
545:         verified: &Verified<pb::JobManifest>,
546:         submitted_at_unix_ms: u64,
547:         fault: Option<TestFault>,
548:     ) -> Result<ManifestBoundSubmitResult, JobStoreError> {
549:         validate_submission(submission)?;
550: 
551:         // This is the first point at which Manifest fields are observed: the
552:         // only Manifest parameter is already wrapped in Verified.
553:         let manifest = verified.get();
554:         validate_manifest_identity(submission, manifest, verified.signer_id())?;
555:         let derived_hash = derive_manifest_hash(manifest);
556:         if submission.manifest_hash != derived_hash {
557:             return Err(JobStoreError::ManifestHashMismatch);
558:         }
559:         let manifest_body = manifest.encode_to_vec();
560: 
561:         let transaction = self
562:             .connection
563:             .transaction_with_behavior(TransactionBehavior::Immediate)
564:             .map_err(map_sql_error)?;
565: 
566:         let mapped_job_id = transaction
567:             .query_row(
568:                 "SELECT job_id FROM job_submission_idempotency WHERE idempotency_key = ?1",
569:                 rusqlite::params![submission.idempotency_key.as_slice()],
570:                 |row| row.get::<_, String>(0),
571:             )
572:             .optional()
573:             .map_err(map_sql_error)?;
574: 
575:         if let Some(stored_job_id) = mapped_job_id {
576:             let stored = fetch_job(&transaction, &stored_job_id)?.ok_or_else(|| {
577:                 JobStoreError::CorruptData(format!(
578:                     "idempotency key points to missing job: {stored_job_id}"
579:                 ))
580:             })?;
581:             if !stored.matches_submission(submission) {
582:                 return Err(JobStoreError::IdempotencyConflict {
583:                     stored_job_id,
584:                     requested_job_id: submission.job_id.clone(),
585:                 });
586:             }
587:             let binding = fetch_manifest_binding(&transaction, &stored)?;
588:             if binding.manifest != *manifest
589:                 || binding.signer_id_at_submission != verified.signer_id()
590:             {
591:                 return Err(JobStoreError::IdempotencyConflict {
592:                     stored_job_id: stored.job_id.clone(),
593:                     requested_job_id: submission.job_id.clone(),
594:                 });
595:             }
596:             transaction.commit().map_err(map_sql_error)?;
597:             return Ok(ManifestBoundSubmitResult {
598:                 job: stored,
599:                 binding,
600:                 created: false,
601:             });
602:         }
603: 
604:         if fetch_job(&transaction, &submission.job_id)?.is_some() {
605:             return Err(JobStoreError::JobIdConflict {
606:                 job_id: submission.job_id.clone(),
607:             });
608:         }
609: 
610:         let stored = StoredJob {
611:             job_id: submission.job_id.clone(),
612:             submitter_device_id: submission.submitter_device_id.clone(),
613:             manifest_hash: derived_hash,
614:             state: JobState::Submitted,
615:             submitted_at_unix_ms,
616:             planning_at_unix_ms: None,
617:             queued_at_unix_ms: None,
618:             staging_at_unix_ms: None,
619:             deadline_unix_ms: submission.deadline_unix_ms,
620:             max_queue_duration_ms: submission.max_queue_duration_ms,
621:             plan_id: None,
622:             queue_failure: None,
623:             failed_at_unix_ms: None,
624:             revision: 0,
625:         };
626: 
627:         insert_job(&transaction, &stored)?;
628:         transaction
629:             .execute(
630:                 "INSERT INTO coordinator_job_manifests(
631:                     job_id, verified_signer_id, manifest_body
632:                  ) VALUES (?1, ?2, ?3)",
633:                 rusqlite::params![stored.job_id, verified.signer_id(), manifest_body],
634:             )
635:             .map_err(map_sql_error)?;
636:         fail_at(fault, TestFault::AfterManifestInsert)?;
637:         transaction
638:             .execute(
639:                 "INSERT INTO job_submission_idempotency(idempotency_key, job_id) VALUES (?1, ?2)",
640:                 rusqlite::params![submission.idempotency_key.as_slice(), stored.job_id],
641:             )
642:             .map_err(map_sql_error)?;
643:         transaction.commit().map_err(map_sql_error)?;
644: 
645:         Ok(ManifestBoundSubmitResult {
646:             job: stored,
647:             binding: StoredManifestBinding {
648:                 manifest: manifest.clone(),
649:                 manifest_hash: derived_hash,
650:                 signer_id_at_submission: verified.signer_id().to_string(),
651:             },
652:             created: true,
881: fn fetch_manifest_binding(
882:     connection: &Connection,
883:     job: &StoredJob,
884: ) -> Result<StoredManifestBinding, JobStoreError> {
885:     let row = connection
886:         .query_row(
887:             "SELECT verified_signer_id, manifest_body
888:              FROM coordinator_job_manifests WHERE job_id = ?1",
889:             rusqlite::params![job.job_id],
890:             |row| Ok((row.get::<_, String>(0)?, row.get::<_, Vec<u8>>(1)?)),
891:         )
892:         .optional()
893:         .map_err(map_sql_error)?
894:         .ok_or_else(|| JobStoreError::LegacyManifestMissing {
895:             job_id: job.job_id.clone(),
896:         })?;
897:     let (signer_id_at_submission, body) = row;
898:     if body.is_empty() {
899:         return Err(manifest_corrupt(job, ManifestCorruption::EmptyBody));
900:     }
901:     let manifest = pb::JobManifest::decode(body.as_slice())
902:         .map_err(|_| manifest_corrupt(job, ManifestCorruption::UndecodableBody))?;
903:     if manifest.job_id != job.job_id {
904:         return Err(manifest_corrupt(job, ManifestCorruption::JobIdMismatch));
905:     }
906:     if manifest.submitter_device_id != job.submitter_device_id {
907:         return Err(manifest_corrupt(
908:             job,
909:             ManifestCorruption::SubmitterDeviceIdMismatch,
910:         ));
911:     }
912:     if signer_id_at_submission != job.submitter_device_id {
913:         return Err(manifest_corrupt(job, ManifestCorruption::SignerIdMismatch));
914:     }
915:     let derived_hash = derive_manifest_hash(&manifest);
916:     if derived_hash != job.manifest_hash {
917:         return Err(manifest_corrupt(job, ManifestCorruption::HashMismatch));
918:     }
919:     Ok(StoredManifestBinding {
920:         manifest,
921:         manifest_hash: derived_hash,
922:         signer_id_at_submission,
923:     })
924: }
925: 
926: fn manifest_corrupt(job: &StoredJob, kind: ManifestCorruption) -> JobStoreError {
927:     JobStoreError::ManifestCorrupt {
928:         job_id: job.job_id.clone(),
929:         kind,
930:     }
931: }

935:         Err(JobStoreError::ClockRollback {
936:             earlier_unix_ms: event,
937:             later_unix_ms: prior,
938:         })
1010: struct RawJobRow {
1011:     job_id: String,
1012:     submitter_device_id: String,
1013:     manifest_hash: Vec<u8>,
1014:     state: String,
1015:     submitted_at_unix_ms: Vec<u8>,
1016:     planning_at_unix_ms: Option<Vec<u8>>,
1017:     queued_at_unix_ms: Option<Vec<u8>>,
1018:     staging_at_unix_ms: Option<Vec<u8>>,
1019:     deadline_unix_ms: Option<Vec<u8>>,
1020:     max_queue_duration_ms: Option<Vec<u8>>,
1021:     plan_id: Option<String>,
1022:     queue_failure_kind: Option<String>,
1023:     queue_failure_detail: Option<String>,
1024:     failed_at_unix_ms: Option<Vec<u8>>,
1025:     revision: Vec<u8>,
1026: }
1027: 
1028: impl RawJobRow {
1029:     fn into_stored(self) -> Result<StoredJob, JobStoreError> {
1030:         let state = JobState::parse(&self.state)?;
1031:         let queue_failure = match self.queue_failure_kind {
1032:             Some(kind) => Some(QueueFailure::parse(&kind, self.queue_failure_detail)?),
1033:             None if self.queue_failure_detail.is_none() => None,
1034:             None => {
1035:                 return Err(JobStoreError::CorruptData(
1036:                     "queue failure detail exists without a kind".to_string(),
1037:                 ))
1038:             }
1039:         };
1040:         let valid_shape = match state {
1041:             JobState::Submitted => {
1042:                 self.planning_at_unix_ms.is_none()
1043:                     && self.queued_at_unix_ms.is_none()
1044:                     && self.staging_at_unix_ms.is_none()
1045:                     && self.plan_id.is_none()
1046:                     && queue_failure.is_none()
1047:                     && self.failed_at_unix_ms.is_none()
1048:             }
1049:             JobState::Planning => {
1050:                 self.planning_at_unix_ms.is_some()
1051:                     && self.queued_at_unix_ms.is_none()
1052:                     && self.staging_at_unix_ms.is_none()
1053:                     && self.plan_id.is_none()
1054:                     && queue_failure.is_none()
1055:                     && self.failed_at_unix_ms.is_none()
1056:             }
1057:             JobState::Queued => {
1058:                 self.planning_at_unix_ms.is_some()
1059:                     && self.queued_at_unix_ms.is_some()
1060:                     && self.staging_at_unix_ms.is_none()
1061:                     && self
1062:                         .plan_id
1063:                         .as_deref()
1064:                         .is_some_and(|plan_id| !plan_id.trim().is_empty())
1065:                     && queue_failure.is_none()
1066:                     && self.failed_at_unix_ms.is_none()
1067:             }
1068:             JobState::Staging => {
1069:                 self.planning_at_unix_ms.is_some()
1070:                     && self.queued_at_unix_ms.is_some()
1071:                     && self.staging_at_unix_ms.is_some()
1072:                     && self
1073:                         .plan_id
1074:                         .as_deref()
1075:                         .is_some_and(|plan_id| !plan_id.trim().is_empty())
1076:                     && queue_failure.is_none()
1077:                     && self.failed_at_unix_ms.is_none()
1078:             }
1079:             JobState::Failed => {
1080:                 self.planning_at_unix_ms.is_some()
1081:                     && self.queued_at_unix_ms.is_some()
1082:                     && self.staging_at_unix_ms.is_none()
1083:                     && self
1084:                         .plan_id
1085:                         .as_deref()
1086:                         .is_some_and(|plan_id| !plan_id.trim().is_empty())
1087:                     && queue_failure.is_some()
1088:                     && self.failed_at_unix_ms.is_some()
1089:             }
1090:         };
1091:         if !valid_shape {
1092:             return Err(JobStoreError::CorruptData(format!(
1093:                 "{} row shape does not match its state",
1094:                 state.as_str()
1095:             )));
1096:         }
1097:         Ok(StoredJob {
1098:             job_id: self.job_id,
1099:             submitter_device_id: self.submitter_device_id,
1100:             manifest_hash: decode_hash(self.manifest_hash)?,
1101:             state,
1102:             submitted_at_unix_ms: decode_u64(&self.submitted_at_unix_ms, "submitted_at_unix_ms")?,
1103:             planning_at_unix_ms: self
1104:                 .planning_at_unix_ms
1105:                 .as_deref()
1106:                 .map(|bytes| decode_u64(bytes, "planning_at_unix_ms"))
1107:                 .transpose()?,
1108:             queued_at_unix_ms: self
1109:                 .queued_at_unix_ms
1110:                 .as_deref()
1111:                 .map(|bytes| decode_u64(bytes, "queued_at_unix_ms"))
1112:                 .transpose()?,
1113:             staging_at_unix_ms: self
1114:                 .staging_at_unix_ms
1115:                 .as_deref()
1116:                 .map(|bytes| decode_u64(bytes, "staging_at_unix_ms"))
1117:                 .transpose()?,
1118:             deadline_unix_ms: self
1119:                 .deadline_unix_ms
1120:                 .as_deref()
1121:                 .map(|bytes| decode_u64(bytes, "deadline_unix_ms"))
1122:                 .transpose()?,
1123:             max_queue_duration_ms: self
1124:                 .max_queue_duration_ms
1125:                 .as_deref()
1126:                 .map(|bytes| decode_u64(bytes, "max_queue_duration_ms"))
1127:                 .transpose()?,
1128:             plan_id: self.plan_id,
1129:             queue_failure,
1130:             failed_at_unix_ms: self
1131:                 .failed_at_unix_ms
1132:                 .as_deref()
1133:                 .map(|bytes| decode_u64(bytes, "failed_at_unix_ms"))
1134:                 .transpose()?,
1135:             revision: decode_u64(&self.revision, "revision")?,
1136:         })
1137:     }
1138: }

61: /// ȣ��ΰ� ���ϴ� ��. **�� ����� �ð踦 ���� �ʴ´�.**

64: ///   �Է����ε� Grant �� �޶��� �� �ִ�. �ð��� �����ϰ� ����� �����ϱ� ����
65: ///   ���밪�� ���ڷ� �޴´�(`stage-job` ���� ���� �׽�Ʈ�� �޶��� ��ʸ� ������
66: ///   ��Ҵ�. ��˼� 30��31 ? �Ѷ� "�׽�Ʈ�� ������ �� ����" �� �����µ�, �ð� �뿪��
67: ///   �����ϸ� �׽�Ʈ�� �� �ִ�).
68: #[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
69: pub struct StoredGrantRequest {
70:     pub job_id: String,
71:     pub attempt_id: String,
72:     pub lease_id: String,
73:     pub grant_id: String,
74:     pub issued_at_unix_ms: u64,

76:     /// �� **nonce �� ����� ����� �ƴ϶� ���� ����̴�.**
77:     ///

79:     ///   �׷����� wire �� ���� �� Agent �� �ź��ߴ� ?
80:     ///   `GRANT_REJECTED: nonce does not match connection attempt`.
81:     ///   Agent �� **���� �õ� ��ȣ**���� ������ ���� ����Ѵ�.
82:     ///

84:     ///     �� ���Ϸ� �� ��(`issue-grant`) ? ���� ������ ����.
85:     ///       ���� �Է��̸� ���� Grant ���� ��߱��� ����ϴ�.
86:     ///     �� wire �� ���� �� ? Agent �� ���ǵ� �������� ��� �Ѵ�.
87:     ///

89:     ///   ������ �ٸ� �Һ��ڰ� ������ ������.
90:     pub nonce: Vec<u8>,
91: }

93: /// ����� ���� Grant �� ������ ���� ���� ? ����� ��ֿ� �źθ� **Ÿ������** ������(���� 104, ��˼� 62).
94: ///
95: /// �� ������ ���� ���ڿ��̾��� Coordinator �� ���� Protocol �� �����ߴ� ? ����Ҹ� **�дٰ�** �� ���(�ջ�� �� �� I/O �� �� ����)��
96: ///   Protocol �� ��, ������ ������ �ջ�� ����Ҹ� �ΰ� ���� ������ �޾Ҵ�.
97: #[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
98: pub enum StoredGrantError {
99:     /// ����Ҹ� �дٰ� �� ��� ? ȣ���ڴ� fail-closed(Storage) �Ѵ�.
100:     Storage(String),
101:     /// ����� ��ǰ� ��û�� ���� �ʾ� ������ �ʴ´�(GRANT_REFUSED �� �ð� �� Manifest ����� ��) ? �� ��û���� �źδ�.
102:     Refused(String),
103: }
104: 
105: impl std::fmt::Display for StoredGrantError {
106:     fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
107:         match self {
108:             Self::Storage(message) | Self::Refused(message) => f.write_str(message),
109:         }
110:     }
111: }
112: 
113: // �� ���� 112 (��˼� 64) ? `From<String> for StoredGrantError`(= Refused)�� ���� �ʴ´�. ������ ����� ȣ�⿡ `format!` ���� ���� `?` ��
114: //   ���ص� �����ϵǸ鼭 �źη� �з��ȴ�. �������� `Refused` �� `Storage` �� ���� �� �з� ������ ������ ������ �����.
115: 
116: /// ���Ϸ� ���� ���(`issue-grant`)�� ������ ����.
117: impl From<StoredGrantError> for String {
118:     fn from(error: StoredGrantError) -> Self {
119:         error.to_string()
120:     }
121: }

123: /// ����� ������ �о� ������ �� ������ Grant �� �����.
124: ///
125: /// nested Lease �� **����** �ϼ��� �����Ѵ� ? �׷��� outer Grant �� ������
126: /// ���� nested ����Ʈ�� ���´�.
127: ///
128: /// ������ ���� Manifest �� `submitters` �� **�߱� �ð� ����** �ٽ� ������ ��
129: /// �ƴ´�(��� ���� "Manifest �� �ƴ´�").
130: pub fn signed_grant_from_stored<K: KeyDirectory + ?Sized>(
131:     jobs: &CoordinatorJobStore,
132:     staging: &CoordinatorStagingStore,
133:     leases: &CoordinatorLeaseStore,
134:     request: &StoredGrantRequest,
135:     key: &SigningKey,
136:     submitters: &K,
137: ) -> Result<pb::ExecutionGrant, StoredGrantError> {
138:     if request.issued_at_unix_ms >= request.expires_at_unix_ms {
139:         return Err(StoredGrantError::Refused(format!(
140:             "Grant �߱� �ð�({})�� ����({}) ���� �ռ��� �ʴ´�",
141:             request.issued_at_unix_ms, request.expires_at_unix_ms
142:         )));
143:     }
144: 
145:     let job = jobs
146:         .get(&request.job_id)
147:         .map_err(|e| StoredGrantError::Storage(format!("Job ��ȸ ����: {e}")))?
148:         .ok_or_else(|| StoredGrantError::Refused(format!("GRANT_REFUSED: {} �� �𸥴�", request.job_id)))?;
149:     if job.state != JobState::Staging {
150:         return Err(StoredGrantError::Refused(format!(
151:             "GRANT_REFUSED: Job �� STAGING �� �ƴϴ�(���� {:?}) ? ���� ���� Grant �� ������ �ʴ´�",
152:             job.state
153:         )));
154:     }
155: 
156:     let attempt = staging
157:         .get_attempt(&request.attempt_id)
158:         .map_err(|e| StoredGrantError::Storage(format!("Attempt ��ȸ ����: {e}")))?
159:         .ok_or_else(|| {
160:             StoredGrantError::Refused(format!(
161:                 "GRANT_REFUSED: Attempt {} �� ����ҿ� ����",
162:                 request.attempt_id
163:             ))
164:         })?;
165:     let stored_lease = leases
166:         .get(&request.lease_id)
167:         .map_err(|e| StoredGrantError::Storage(format!("Lease ��ȸ ����: {e}")))?
168:         .ok_or_else(|| StoredGrantError::Refused(format!("GRANT_REFUSED: Lease {} �� ����ҿ� ����", request.lease_id)))?;
169: 
170:     // ���� �� �ุ ���� �ʴ´� ����������������������������������������������������������������������������������
171:     for (label, left, right) in [
172:         (
173:             "job_id",
174:             attempt.job_id.as_str(),
175:             stored_lease.job_id.as_str(),
176:         ),
177:         (
178:             "attempt_id",
179:             attempt.attempt_id.as_str(),
180:             stored_lease.attempt_id.as_str(),
181:         ),
182:         (
183:             "lease_id",
184:             attempt.lease_id.as_str(),
185:             stored_lease.lease_id.as_str(),
186:         ),
187:     ] {
188:         if left != right {
189:             return Err(StoredGrantError::Refused(format!(
190:                 "GRANT_REFUSED: Attempt �� Lease �� {label} �� �ٸ���(Attempt {left:?}, Lease {right:?}) ? �� ���� ���� ������ ����Ű�� �ʴ´�"
191:             )));
192:         }
193:     }
194:     // �ڡ� **�Ʒ� �� ������ �׽�Ʈ�� �������� �ʾҴ� ? ������ ���´�.**
195:     //
196:     //   �����̼� G5(fence ���� ����)��G6(���� ���� Ȯ�� ����)�� �ɾ
197:     //   `issue_grant` 10���� ���� ����Ѵ�. ������ �����Ų �� �ƴ϶�
198:     //   **�� ���¸� ���� ��η� ���� �� ���**�� ? Attempt��Lease��
199:     //   ������ `staging_store.rs` �� **�� `BEGIN IMMEDIATE` �ȿ��� �Բ�**
200:     //   ���δ�(`:605`��`:915`��`:1985`). ���� ��߳����� DB �� ����
201:     //   �����ؾ� �Ѵ�.
202:     //
203:     //   �׷��� �̰� **�Է� ������ �ƴ϶� �ջ� ���**��. ����ġ�� ���ٴ�
204:     //   ���� �ƴ�����, "�׽�Ʈ�� ��Ű�� �ִ�" �� ���ϸ� �����̴�.
205:     //   �����Ϸ��� rusqlite �� ���� ���� �����߸��� �׽�Ʈ�� �ʿ��ϴ�.
206:     //   �� 2026-09-17 ���� ? ���� "�� crate �� ���� �׽�Ʈ���� �� �������� ����" �� ���� �־���. Ʋ�ȴ� ?
207:     //     `tests/attempt_report_ingress.rs` �� rusqlite �� ���� �����߸���(���� 104 �� 111). �� �� ������ �����ϴ� �׽�Ʈ�� ���� ����.
208:     if attempt.fence_epoch != stored_lease.fence_epoch {
209:         return Err(StoredGrantError::Refused(format!(
210:             "GRANT_REFUSED: Attempt �� Lease �� fence epoch �� �ٸ���(Attempt {}, Lease {}) ? ������ �������� Grant �� ����� fencing �� ���ǹ�������",
211:             attempt.fence_epoch, stored_lease.fence_epoch
212:         )));
213:     }
214:     if attempt.job_id != request.job_id {
215:         return Err(StoredGrantError::Refused(format!(
216:             "GRANT_REFUSED: Attempt �� �ٸ� Job �� ���̴�({} != {})",
217:             attempt.job_id, request.job_id
218:         )));
219:     }
220: 
221:     let reservation = staging
222:         .get_node_reservation(&stored_lease.holder_node_id)
223:         .map_err(|e| StoredGrantError::Storage(format!("���� ��ȸ ����: {e}")))?
224:         .ok_or_else(|| {
225:             StoredGrantError::Refused(format!(
226:                 "GRANT_REFUSED: {} �� ������ ���� ? Lease �� �ִµ� ��尡 �� ���� �ִ�",
227:                 stored_lease.holder_node_id
228:             ))
229:         })?;
230:     if reservation.job_id != request.job_id || reservation.attempt_id != request.attempt_id {
231:         return Err(StoredGrantError::Refused(format!(
232:             "GRANT_REFUSED: {} �� ������ �ٸ� Attempt �� ���̴�(job {}, attempt {})",
233:             stored_lease.holder_node_id, reservation.job_id, reservation.attempt_id
234:         )));
235:     }
236: 
237:     // ���� ���� ���� ������������������������������������������������������������������������������������������������������
238:     if let Some(revoked_at) = stored_lease.revoked_at_unix_ms {
239:         return Err(StoredGrantError::Refused(format!(
240:             "GRANT_REFUSED: Lease �� {revoked_at} �� ���ƴ� ? ���� Lease �� Grant �� ������ �ʴ´�"
241:         )));
242:     }
243:     // �� ��� ����(`<=`) ? `DoD-26`��`DoD-32`��`DoD-34` �� ������Ų ��Ģ��
244:     //   ����. ��Ȯ�� ���� �ð��� Lease �� ���⼭�� ��ȿ�� ����, Agent ��
245:     //   ���� ���� �� Lease �� �ź��Ѵ�.
246:     if stored_lease.expires_at_unix_ms <= request.issued_at_unix_ms {
247:         return Err(StoredGrantError::Refused(format!(
248:             "GRANT_REFUSED: �߱� �ð�({})�� Lease �� �̹� �����(���� {})",
249:             request.issued_at_unix_ms, stored_lease.expires_at_unix_ms
250:         )));
251:     }
252:     if request.expires_at_unix_ms > stored_lease.expires_at_unix_ms {
253:         return Err(StoredGrantError::Refused(format!(
254:             "GRANT_REFUSED: Grant ����({})�� Lease ����({}) ���� �ʴ�",
255:             request.expires_at_unix_ms, stored_lease.expires_at_unix_ms
256:         )));
257:     }
258: 
259:     // ���� ������ ���� Manifest ? �Ʊ� ���� **����** �ٽ� �����Ѵ� ������������
260:     //
261:     // ����Ұ� ���� ��(������job_id�������ڡ������ڡ�hash ��ġ)�� �ٽ� ���� �ʴ´� ?
262:     // ��߳��� `get_manifest_binding()` �� ������ ����.
263:     let binding = jobs
264:         .get_manifest_binding(&request.job_id)
265:         // ����� Manifest �� ���� ���� ���� ����� ��ִ�(���� 104) ? ���� ���� �źδ�.
266:         // �� ���� 111 (��˼� 64) ? Job �� �ְ� Manifest �ุ ���� �� hash-only Job �� `Ok(None)` �� �ƴ϶� `LegacyManifestMissing` �̴�.
267:         //   �װͱ��� Storage �� ���� �� Job �ϳ��� �����ʸ� �����. ���� �ջ� �� I/O �� ���� �״�� Storage ��.
268:         .map_err(|e| match e {
269:             JobStoreError::LegacyManifestMissing { job_id } => StoredGrantError::Refused(format!(
270:                 "GRANT_REFUSED: {job_id} �� ������ Manifest ���� ����� �� Job �̴� ? ���� Manifest �� ����"
271:             )),
272:             other => StoredGrantError::Storage(format!("����� Manifest �� ���� ���ߴ�: {other}")),
273:         })?
274:         .ok_or_else(|| {
275:             StoredGrantError::Refused(format!(
276:                 "GRANT_REFUSED: {} �� ����� Manifest �� ����",
277:                 request.job_id
278:             ))
279:         })?;
280:     let verified = gputeer_protocol::verify(
281:         &binding.manifest,
282:         1,
283:         &Ed25519Verifier::new(submitters),
284:         request.issued_at_unix_ms,
285:         &mut gputeer_protocol::signing::NoReplayCheck,
286:     )
287:     .map_err(|e| {
288:         StoredGrantError::Refused(format!(
289:             "GRANT_REFUSED: ����� Manifest �� ���� �ٽ� �������� ���ߴ�: {e:?} ? ����� ���� ��ȿ�ߴ��� �� ���� �����ڰ� �ŷ� ��Ͽ��� �����ų� Manifest �� ������� �� �ִ�"
290:         ))
291:     })?;
292:     // �� Agent �� **�޴� �ð�**���� Manifest �� �����Ѵ�. Grant �� Manifest ����
293:     //   ���� ��� Grant �� ��ȿ�ѵ� Manifest �� ����� ������ �����(���� ���� 36).
294:     //   Lease ���� ���� ������� ���´�.
295:     let manifest_expires_at = verified.get().expires_at_unix_ms;
296:     if request.expires_at_unix_ms > manifest_expires_at {
297:         return Err(StoredGrantError::Refused(format!(
298:             "GRANT_REFUSED: Grant ����({})�� Manifest ����({manifest_expires_at}) ���� �ʴ�",
299:             request.expires_at_unix_ms
300:         )));
301:     }
302: 
303:     // ���� ���� ����������������������������������������������������������������������������������������������������������������
304:     // ���尪�� wire ������ �ű��� ���� ��(u64 -> u32 ����)�� ����� �ջ��̴�(���� 104).
305:     let mut lease = unsigned_lease_from_stored(&stored_lease).map_err(StoredGrantError::Storage)?;
306:     lease.coordinator_signature = sign(key, &lease).to_vec();
307: 
308:     let mut grant = pb::ExecutionGrant {
309:         schema_version: 2,
310:         grant_id: request.grant_id.clone(),
311:         attempt_id: attempt.attempt_id.clone(),
312:         coordinator_device_id: stored_lease.issuing_coordinator_id.clone(),
313:         // �� ���ͷ� 1 �� �ƴ϶� ����� term �̴�. `coordinator-stub` ��
314:         //   ���� `issue_grant()` �� ���⿡ 1 �� �ھ� �ִ´�.
315:         coordinator_term: stored_lease.coordinator_term,
316:         issued_at_unix_ms: request.issued_at_unix_ms,
317:         expires_at_unix_ms: request.expires_at_unix_ms,
318:         nonce: request.nonce.clone(),
319:         // �� ��Ʈ�� v2 Grant ���� ����̴�. �� ��δ� **����ҿ�����**
320:         // Lease �� �����Ƿ� �����ϰ� true ��.
321:         lease_from_durable_store: true,
322:         lease: Some(lease),
323:         // �� outer ���� **����** �ƴ´� ? �׷��� Grant ������ ���� nested ����Ʈ��
324:         //   hash �� ���´�. hash �� ����Ұ� ������ ������ ���� �״�� ����.

382: ///   directory �� ���� ȣ��ΰ� �� Ű�� �״�� ����. �װ� ���� �˻簡
383: ///   �ʿ��ϰ� �� ����ҿ� ���� ����.
384: fn verify_own_output(
385:     grant: &pb::ExecutionGrant,
386:     key: &gputeer_crypto::SigningKey,
387:     at_unix_ms: u64,
388: ) -> Result<(), String> {
389:     // ������ �� Ű�� ����Ű�� �� �ſ� ��ο� ����Ѵ� ? Grant �� nested
390:     // Lease �� ������ �̸��� �ٸ� �� �ִµ�, ���⼭ Ȯ���Ϸ��� ����
391:     // "�� Ű�� ������ ���� �״�� �����Ǵ°�" ���̴�.
392:     let mut ring = gputeer_crypto::InMemoryKeyring::new();
393:     let public = key.verifying_key();
394:     ring.insert(grant.coordinator_device_id.clone(), public);
395:     if let Some(lease) = grant.lease.as_ref() {
396:         ring.insert(lease.issuing_coordinator_id.clone(), public);
397:     }
398:     let verifier = gputeer_crypto::Ed25519Verifier::new(&ring);
399: 
400:     gputeer_protocol::verify(
401:         grant,
402:         2,
403:         &verifier,
404:         at_unix_ms,
405:         &mut gputeer_protocol::signing::NoReplayCheck,
406:     )
407:     .map_err(|e| format!("��� ���� Grant �� �ڱ� ������ ������� ���ߴ�: {e:?}"))?;
408: 
409:     let lease = grant
410:         .lease
411:         .as_ref()
412:         .ok_or_else(|| "��� ���� Grant �� Lease �� ����".to_string())?;
413:     gputeer_protocol::verify(
414:         lease,
415:         1,
416:         &verifier,
417:         at_unix_ms,
418:         &mut gputeer_protocol::signing::NoReplayCheck,
419:     )
420:     .map_err(|e| format!("��� ���� nested Lease �� �ڱ� ������ ������� ���ߴ�: {e:?}"))?;
421:     Ok(())
422: }

432: pub fn derive_stored_grant_nonce(grant_id: &str, attempt_id: &str) -> Vec<u8> {
433:     let mut input = Vec::new();
434:     for part in [
435:         b"gputeer/v1/grant-nonce".as_slice(),

440:         input.extend_from_slice(part);
441:     }

443: }

165: /// ����Ű�� **���Ͽ�����** �д´�. �����ٿ� ������ �ʴ� ���� �����̴�.
166: fn load_signing_key(path: &str) -> Result<SigningKey, String> {
167:     let raw = std::fs::read_to_string(path)
168:         .map_err(|e| format!("����Ű ������ ���� ���ߴ�({path}): {e}"))?;
169:     let hex = raw.trim();
170:     if hex.len() != 64 {
171:         return Err(format!(
172:             "����Ű ������ 64�ڸ� hex(32����Ʈ) �ϳ����� �Ѵ�({path}, ���� {})",
173:             hex.len()
174:         ));
175:     }
176:     // �� ���� ��(2026-09-10) ? ����Ʈ�� �ڸ��� �ʴ´�. `gputeer_crypto::hex`
177:     //   �� �� ����Ʈ�� �����Ƿ� ���� ��踦 ���� �� ����.
178:     let seed = gputeer_crypto::hex::decode_fixed::<32>(hex).map_err(|e| {
179:         format!("GRANT_REFUSED: KEY_NOT_HEX ? ����Ű hex �Ľ� ����({path}): {e}")
180:     })?;
181:     Ok(SigningKey::from_bytes(&seed))
182: }

415:     /// Returns the durable queue in deterministic FIFO order. `job_id` is the
435:     pub fn submit_accepted(
436:         &mut self,
437:         submission: &AcceptedJobSubmission,
438:         submitted_at_unix_ms: u64,
439:     ) -> Result<SubmitResult, JobStoreError> {
440:         validate_submission(submission)?;
441:         let transaction = self
442:             .connection
443:             .transaction_with_behavior(TransactionBehavior::Immediate)
444:             .map_err(map_sql_error)?;
445: 
446:         let mapped_job_id = transaction
447:             .query_row(
448:                 "SELECT job_id FROM job_submission_idempotency WHERE idempotency_key = ?1",
449:                 rusqlite::params![submission.idempotency_key.as_slice()],
450:                 |row| row.get::<_, String>(0),
451:             )
452:             .optional()
453:             .map_err(map_sql_error)?;
454: 
455:         if let Some(stored_job_id) = mapped_job_id {
456:             let stored = fetch_job(&transaction, &stored_job_id)?.ok_or_else(|| {
457:                 JobStoreError::CorruptData(format!(
458:                     "idempotency key points to missing job: {stored_job_id}"
459:                 ))
460:             })?;
461:             if !stored.matches_submission(submission) {
462:                 return Err(JobStoreError::IdempotencyConflict {
463:                     stored_job_id,
464:                     requested_job_id: submission.job_id.clone(),
465:                 });
466:             }
467:             transaction.commit().map_err(map_sql_error)?;
468:             return Ok(SubmitResult {
469:                 job: stored,
470:                 created: false,
471:             });
472:         }
473: 
474:         if fetch_job(&transaction, &submission.job_id)?.is_some() {
475:             return Err(JobStoreError::JobIdConflict {
476:                 job_id: submission.job_id.clone(),
477:             });
478:         }
479: 
480:         let stored = StoredJob {
481:             job_id: submission.job_id.clone(),
482:             submitter_device_id: submission.submitter_device_id.clone(),
483:             manifest_hash: submission.manifest_hash,
484:             state: JobState::Submitted,
485:             submitted_at_unix_ms,
486:             planning_at_unix_ms: None,
487:             queued_at_unix_ms: None,
488:             staging_at_unix_ms: None,
489:             deadline_unix_ms: submission.deadline_unix_ms,
490:             max_queue_duration_ms: submission.max_queue_duration_ms,
491:             plan_id: None,
492:             queue_failure: None,
493:             failed_at_unix_ms: None,
494:             revision: 0,
495:         };
496: 
497:         transaction
498:             .execute(
499:                 "INSERT INTO coordinator_jobs(
500:                     job_id, submitter_device_id, manifest_hash, state,
501:                     submitted_at_unix_ms, planning_at_unix_ms, queued_at_unix_ms, staging_at_unix_ms,
502:                     deadline_unix_ms, max_queue_duration_ms, plan_id,
503:                     queue_failure_kind, queue_failure_detail, failed_at_unix_ms, revision
504:                  ) VALUES (?1, ?2, ?3, ?4, ?5, NULL, NULL, NULL, ?6, ?7, NULL, NULL, NULL, NULL, ?8)",
505:                 rusqlite::params![
506:                     stored.job_id,
507:                     stored.submitter_device_id,
508:                     stored.manifest_hash.as_slice(),
509:                     stored.state.as_str(),
510:                     encode_u64(stored.submitted_at_unix_ms),
511:                     stored.deadline_unix_ms.map(encode_u64),
512:                     stored.max_queue_duration_ms.map(encode_u64),
513:                     encode_u64(stored.revision),
514:                 ],
515:             )
516:             .map_err(map_sql_error)?;
517:         transaction
518:             .execute(
519:                 "INSERT INTO job_submission_idempotency(idempotency_key, job_id) VALUES (?1, ?2)",
520:                 rusqlite::params![submission.idempotency_key.as_slice(), stored.job_id],
521:             )
522:             .map_err(map_sql_error)?;
523:         transaction.commit().map_err(map_sql_error)?;
524:         Ok(SubmitResult {
525:             job: stored,
526:             created: true,
527:         })
528:     }
529: 
530:     /// Atomically persists an accepted Job, its idempotency binding, and the
881: fn fetch_manifest_binding(
882:     connection: &Connection,
883:     job: &StoredJob,
884: ) -> Result<StoredManifestBinding, JobStoreError> {
885:     let row = connection
886:         .query_row(
887:             "SELECT verified_signer_id, manifest_body
888:              FROM coordinator_job_manifests WHERE job_id = ?1",
889:             rusqlite::params![job.job_id],
890:             |row| Ok((row.get::<_, String>(0)?, row.get::<_, Vec<u8>>(1)?)),
891:         )
892:         .optional()
893:         .map_err(map_sql_error)?
894:         .ok_or_else(|| JobStoreError::LegacyManifestMissing {
895:             job_id: job.job_id.clone(),
896:         })?;
897:     let (signer_id_at_submission, body) = row;
898:     if body.is_empty() {
899:         return Err(manifest_corrupt(job, ManifestCorruption::EmptyBody));
900:     }
901:     let manifest = pb::JobManifest::decode(body.as_slice())
902:         .map_err(|_| manifest_corrupt(job, ManifestCorruption::UndecodableBody))?;
903:     if manifest.job_id != job.job_id {
904:         return Err(manifest_corrupt(job, ManifestCorruption::JobIdMismatch));
905:     }
906:     if manifest.submitter_device_id != job.submitter_device_id {
907:         return Err(manifest_corrupt(
908:             job,
909:             ManifestCorruption::SubmitterDeviceIdMismatch,
910:         ));
911:     }
912:     if signer_id_at_submission != job.submitter_device_id {
913:         return Err(manifest_corrupt(job, ManifestCorruption::SignerIdMismatch));
914:     }
915:     let derived_hash = derive_manifest_hash(&manifest);
916:     if derived_hash != job.manifest_hash {
917:         return Err(manifest_corrupt(job, ManifestCorruption::HashMismatch));
918:     }
919:     Ok(StoredManifestBinding {
920:         manifest,
921:         manifest_hash: derived_hash,
922:         signer_id_at_submission,
923:     })
924: }
925: 
926: fn manifest_corrupt(job: &StoredJob, kind: ManifestCorruption) -> JobStoreError {
927:     JobStoreError::ManifestCorrupt {
928:         job_id: job.job_id.clone(),
929:         kind,
930:     }
931: }

934:     if event < prior {
1080:                 self.planning_at_unix_ms.is_some()
1081:                     && self.queued_at_unix_ms.is_some()
1082:                     && self.staging_at_unix_ms.is_none()
1083:                     && self
1084:                         .plan_id
1085:                         .as_deref()
1086:                         .is_some_and(|plan_id| !plan_id.trim().is_empty())
1087:                     && queue_failure.is_some()
1088:                     && self.failed_at_unix_ms.is_some()
1089:             }
1090:         };
1091:         if !valid_shape {
1092:             return Err(JobStoreError::CorruptData(format!(
1093:                 "{} row shape does not match its state",
1094:                 state.as_str()
1095:             )));
1096:         }

1117:                 .transpose()?,
1118:             deadline_unix_ms: self
1119:                 .deadline_unix_ms
1120:                 .as_deref()

1122:                 .transpose()?,
1123:             max_queue_duration_ms: self
1124:                 .max_queue_duration_ms

1126:                 .map(|bytes| decode_u64(bytes, "max_queue_duration_ms"))

1128:             plan_id: self.plan_id,

266:         // ★ 결함 111 (재검수 64) — Job 은 있고 Manifest 행만 없는 옛 hash-only Job 은 `Ok(None)` 이 아니라 `LegacyManifestMissing` 이다.
267:         //   그것까지 Storage 로 묶어 옛 Job 하나가 리스너를 멈췄다. 본문 손상 · I/O · 락은 그대로 Storage 다.

293:     //   오래 살면 Grant 는 유효한데 Manifest 는 만료된 구간이 생긴다(설계 논의 36).
294:     //   Lease 경계와 같은 모양으로 막는다.

296:     if request.expires_at_unix_ms > manifest_expires_at {
297:         return Err(StoredGrantError::Refused(format!(

305:     let mut lease = unsigned_lease_from_stored(&stored_lease).map_err(StoredGrantError::Storage)?;
306:     lease.coordinator_signature = sign(key, &lease).to_vec();

308:     let mut grant = pb::ExecutionGrant {
309:         schema_version: 2,
310:         grant_id: request.grant_id.clone(),
311:         attempt_id: attempt.attempt_id.clone(),
312:         coordinator_device_id: stored_lease.issuing_coordinator_id.clone(),
313:         // ★ 리터럴 1 이 아니라 저장된 term 이다. `coordinator-stub` 의
314:         //   기존 `issue_grant()` 는 여기에 1 을 박아 넣는다.
315:         coordinator_term: stored_lease.coordinator_term,
316:         issued_at_unix_ms: request.issued_at_unix_ms,
317:         expires_at_unix_ms: request.expires_at_unix_ms,
318:         nonce: request.nonce.clone(),
319:         // 이 비트는 v2 Grant 서명 대상이다. 이 경로는 **저장소에서만**
320:         // Lease 를 읽으므로 정직하게 true 다.
321:         lease_from_durable_store: true,
322:         lease: Some(lease),
323:         // ★ outer 서명 **전에** 싣는다 — 그래야 Grant 서명이 최종 nested 바이트와
324:         //   hash 를 덮는다. hash 는 저장소가 재계산해 대조한 값을 그대로 쓴다.
325:         manifest_hash: Some(pb::Digest {
326:             algo: 1, // HASH_ALGORITHM_BLAKE3_256
327:             value: binding.manifest_hash.to_vec(),
328:         }),
329:         manifest: Some(binding.manifest),
330:         ..Default::default()
331:     };
332:     grant.coordinator_signature = sign(key, &grant).to_vec();
333: 
334:     // ── 방금 만든 것을 바로 다시 검증한다 ───────────────────────────
335:     //
336:     // ★ `gputeer submit` 이 이미 하는 일이다("서명하자마자 깨진 파일을
337:     //   내보내는 것보다 여기서 실패하는 편이 훨씬 싸다"). 이 경로에는
338:     //   그게 **없었다** — 내 뮤테이션이 아니라 코드를 나란히 놓고 보다가
339:     //   찾았다.
340:     //
341:     //   확인하는 것과 못 하는 것을 정확히 적는다:
342:     //
343:     //   확인한다   구조·`schema_version`·수명·canonical 인코딩,
344:     //              그리고 **서명이 실제로 검증되는가**(2026-09-07 추가).
345:     //              nested Lease 도 같이 본다.
346:     //   못 한다    **이 키가 정말 `issuing_coordinator_id` 의 것인가.**
347:     //              이 모듈에는 key directory 가 없다 — 호출부가 준
348:     //              키를 그대로 쓴다.
349:     //
350:     //   ★★ **"위험하지 않다" 고 적었던 것을 고친다** (2026-09-07 독립
351:     //     검수 지적). 엉뚱한 키로 서명하면 여기서는 통과하고 나중에
352:     //     Agent 가 거부한다. 인증 우회는 아니지만 **위험이 없는 것은
353:     //     아니다**:
354:     //
355:     //       · 운영자는 유효한 Grant 를 만든 줄 안다
356:     //       · 실패가 Agent 단계까지 밀려 원인 파악이 늦어진다
357:     //       · `--out` 에 기존 파일이 있었다면 못 쓰는 Grant 로 덮인다
358:     //
359:     //     닫으려면 Coordinator 공개키 목록이 필요하고 아직 없다.
360:     // 자기 검증 실패는 저장소 장애가 아니다 — 메모리 keyring 으로 보고, 입력 정책(수명 상한 등) 실패도 포함한다(재검수 64).
361:     verify_own_output(&grant, key, request.issued_at_unix_ms).map_err(StoredGrantError::Refused)?;
362:     Ok(grant)
363: }
364: 
365: /// 방금 서명한 Grant 를 **서명까지** 자기 검증한다.
366: ///
367: /// ★★ **2026-09-07 독립 검수가 이 함수를 반려했다.** 전에는
368: ///   `AlwaysValid`(서명을 읽지도 않는 검증자)를 썼다. 검수가 반례를
369: ///   보였다 —
370: ///
371: ///   > `sign()` 이 버그 때문에 잘못된 preimage 를 서명하거나 64바이트
372: ///   > 쓰레기를 반환해도, 검증기가 서명을 **읽지 않으므로** 통과한다.
373: ///
374: ///   즉 그 검사는 "서명 생성과 검증 양쪽에 같은 버그가 있으면 못 잡는다"
375: ///   보다도 약했다. **생성기의 단독 버그조차 못 잡았다.**
376: ///
377: ///   지금은 서명에 쓴 키에서 공개키를 도출해 **실제로 검증한다.**
378: ///   그러면 서명 생성과 canonical preimage 의 불일치를 잡는다.
379: ///
380: /// ★ 여전히 못 하는 것은 그대로다 — **이 공개키가 정말
381: ///   `issuing_coordinator_id` 의 신뢰된 키인가.** 이 모듈에는 key
382: ///   directory 가 없고 호출부가 준 키를 그대로 쓴다. 그건 별도 검사가
383: ///   필요하고 이 저장소에 아직 없다.
384: fn verify_own_output(
385:     grant: &pb::ExecutionGrant,
386:     key: &gputeer_crypto::SigningKey,
387:     at_unix_ms: u64,
388: ) -> Result<(), String> {
389:     // 서명에 쓴 키의 공개키를 두 신원 모두에 등록한다 — Grant 와 nested
390:     // Lease 는 서명자 이름이 다를 수 있는데, 여기서 확인하려는 것은
391:     // "이 키로 서명한 것이 그대로 검증되는가" 뿐이다.
392:     let mut ring = gputeer_crypto::InMemoryKeyring::new();
393:     let public = key.verifying_key();
394:     ring.insert(grant.coordinator_device_id.clone(), public);
395:     if let Some(lease) = grant.lease.as_ref() {
396:         ring.insert(lease.issuing_coordinator_id.clone(), public);
397:     }
398:     let verifier = gputeer_crypto::Ed25519Verifier::new(&ring);
399: 
400:     gputeer_protocol::verify(
401:         grant,
402:         2,
403:         &verifier,
404:         at_unix_ms,
405:         &mut gputeer_protocol::signing::NoReplayCheck,
406:     )
407:     .map_err(|e| format!("방금 만든 Grant 가 자기 검증을 통과하지 못했다: {e:?}"))?;
408: 
409:     let lease = grant
410:         .lease
411:         .as_ref()
412:         .ok_or_else(|| "방금 만든 Grant 에 Lease 가 없다".to_string())?;
413:     gputeer_protocol::verify(
414:         lease,
415:         1,
416:         &verifier,
417:         at_unix_ms,
418:         &mut gputeer_protocol::signing::NoReplayCheck,
419:     )
420:     .map_err(|e| format!("방금 만든 nested Lease 가 자기 검증을 통과하지 못했다: {e:?}"))?;
421:     Ok(())
422: }

70: pub fn run(args: &[String]) -> Result<String, String> {
71:     let flags = parse_flags(args)?;
72: 
73:     let job_id = require(&flags, "--job-id")?;
74:     let control_db = require(&flags, "--control-db")?;
75:     let attempt_id = require(&flags, "--attempt-id")?;
76:     let lease_id = require(&flags, "--lease-id")?;
77:     let grant_id = require(&flags, "--grant-id")?;
78:     let out_path = require(&flags, "--out")?;
79:     // ★ 기본값은 **덮어쓰지 않는다.** 위 `write_grant_file` 주석 참조.
80:     let overwrite = matches!(
81:         flags.get("--overwrite-existing-grant").map(String::as_str),
82:         Some("true")
83:     );
84: 
85:     // ★ Grant 시각도 절대값이다. `stage-job` 에서 배운 것과 같은 이유 —
86:     //   실시간 시각을 결과에 반영하면 같은 명시적 입력으로도 Grant 가 달라질 수
87:     //   있다. 시각을 통제하고 결과를 재현하기 위해 절대값을 인자로 받는다
88:     //   (재검수 30·31 — 한때 "테스트로 고정할 수 없다" 고 적었는데, 시계 대역을
89:     //   고정하면 테스트할 수 있다. 설계 이유를 다른 방법의 불가능으로 넓힌 것이었다).
90:     let issued_at_unix_ms = u64_flag(&flags, "--grant-issued-at-unix-ms")?;
91:     let expires_at_unix_ms = u64_flag(&flags, "--grant-expires-at-unix-ms")?;
92:     if issued_at_unix_ms >= expires_at_unix_ms {

crates/coordinator/src/lib.rs
311: /// A failure isolated to one accepted Coordinator session.
312: ///
313: /// Transport and protocol failures are connection-scoped: the dispatcher logs
314: /// them and returns to `accept()`. A storage failure means the Coordinator can
315: /// no longer make a trustworthy lease decision, so `run()` fails closed.
316: #[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
317: pub enum CoordinatorSessionError {
318:     Transport(String),
319:     Protocol(String),
320:     Storage(String),
321: }
322: 
323: impl std::fmt::Display for CoordinatorSessionError {
324:     fn fmt(&self, formatter: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
325:         match self {
326:             Self::Transport(message) => write!(formatter, "transport: {message}"),
327:             Self::Protocol(message) => write!(formatter, "protocol: {message}"),
328:             Self::Storage(message) => write!(formatter, "storage: {message}"),
329:         }
330:     }
331: }
332: 
333: impl std::error::Error for CoordinatorSessionError {}
334: 
335: fn transport_error(context: &str, error: impl std::fmt::Display) -> CoordinatorSessionError {
336:     CoordinatorSessionError::Transport(format!("{context}: {error}"))
337: }
338: 
339: fn protocol_error(context: &str, error: impl std::fmt::Display) -> CoordinatorSessionError {
340:     CoordinatorSessionError::Protocol(format!("{context}: {error}"))
341: }
342: 
343: fn storage_error(context: &str, error: impl std::fmt::Display) -> CoordinatorSessionError {
344:     CoordinatorSessionError::Storage(format!("{context}: {error}"))
345: }
346: 
347: /// The legacy coordinator handlers still produce string errors for their
348: /// transport/protocol paths. Resume storage failures are different: they must
349: /// reach the dispatcher as a typed `Storage` error instead of being converted
350: /// into a signed UNAVAILABLE result or reclassified by message text.
351: enum SessionHandlerError {
352:     Legacy(String),
353:     Classified(CoordinatorSessionError),
354: }
355: 
356: impl From<String> for SessionHandlerError {
357:     fn from(message: String) -> Self {
358:         Self::Legacy(message)
359:     }
360: }

362: fn classify_resume_store_error(error: LeaseStoreError) -> CoordinatorSessionError {
363:     match error {
364:         // These are the only store failures classify_resume currently emits.
365:         LeaseStoreError::Io(_) | LeaseStoreError::LockTimeout => {
366:             storage_error("resume lease classification", error)
367:         }
368:         // These variants are policy rejections in the issue/renew APIs, not
369:         // valid classify_resume errors (ResumeDecision carries those policy
370:         // outcomes). If one ever crosses this boundary, fail closed.
371:         LeaseStoreError::NotFound
585: 
586: 
587:     println!("READY {address}");
588:     std::io::stdout().flush().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
589: 
590:     let signing_key = SigningKey::from_bytes(&config.own_seed);
591:     let mut agent_keys = InMemoryKeyring::new();
592:     agent_keys.insert(config.agent_device_id.clone(), config.agent_verifying_key);
593: 
594:     let mut replay = InMemoryReplayGuard::new();
595:     let clock = SystemClock;
596: 
597:     listener
598:         .set_nonblocking(true)
599:         .map_err(|e| format!("listener nonblocking failed: {e}"))?;
600:     let mut connection_count = 0u32;
601:     loop {
602:         if connection_count >= config.max_connections {
603:             return Err("max-connections reached before completed session".into());
604:         }
605:         if connection_count > 0 && config.pause_before_next_accept_ms != 0 {
606:             std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(config.pause_before_next_accept_ms));
607:         }
608:         let (mut stream, peer) =
609:             accept_with_deadline(&listener, Duration::from_millis(config.accept_timeout_ms))?;
610:         let connection_attempt = connection_count;
611:         connection_count += 1;
612:         println!("CONNECTION_ATTEMPT {connection_attempt} peer={peer}");
613: 
614:         match serve_one_connection(
615:             &config,
616:             &mut stream,
617:             &mut lease_store,
618:             &mut neighbor_store,
619:             &mut attempt_report_store,
620:             &signing_key,
621:             &agent_keys,
622:             &mut replay,
623:             &clock,
624:             connection_attempt,
625:         ) {
626:             Ok(()) => {
627:                 if connection_count >= config.max_connections {
628:                     return Ok(());
629:                 }
630:             }
631:             Err(error @ CoordinatorSessionError::Transport(_))
632:             | Err(error @ CoordinatorSessionError::Protocol(_)) => {
633:                 eprintln!(
634:                     "SESSION_ERROR peer={peer} connection_attempt={connection_attempt} kind={} error={error}",
635:                     session_error_kind(&error)
636:                 );
637:                 if connection_count >= config.max_connections {
638:                     return Err(error.to_string());
639:                 }
640:             }
641:             Err(error @ CoordinatorSessionError::Storage(_)) => {
642:                 eprintln!(
643:                     "SESSION_ERROR peer={peer} connection_attempt={connection_attempt} kind=storage error={error}"
644:                 );
645:                 return Err(error.to_string());
646:             }
647:         }
648:     }
649: }

1000: /// Dispatch and serve exactly one accepted connection. The implementation is
1001: /// deliberately sequential; the outer loop owns accept/count/error isolation.
1002: #[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
1003: fn serve_one_connection(
1004:     config: &CoordinatorConfig,
1005:     stream: &mut std::net::TcpStream,
1006:     lease_store: &mut Option<CoordinatorLeaseStore>,
1007:     neighbor_store: &mut Option<crate::neighbor_report_store::CoordinatorNeighborReportStore>,
1008:     attempt_report_store: &mut Option<crate::attempt_report_store::CoordinatorAttemptReportStore>,
1009:     signing_key: &SigningKey,
1010:     agent_keys: &InMemoryKeyring,
1011:     replay: &mut InMemoryReplayGuard,
1012:     clock: &SystemClock,
1013:     connection_attempt: u32,
1014: ) -> Result<(), CoordinatorSessionError> {
1015:     stream
1016:         .set_nonblocking(false)
1017:         .map_err(|e| transport_error("accepted stream blocking mode failed", e))?;
1018:     stream
1019:         .set_read_timeout(Some(IO_TIMEOUT))
1020:         .map_err(|e| transport_error("read timeout setup failed", e))?;
1021:     stream
1022:         .set_write_timeout(Some(IO_TIMEOUT))
1023:         .map_err(|e| transport_error("write timeout setup failed", e))?;
1024: 
1025:     serve_one_connection_impl(
1026:         config,
1027:         stream,
1028:         lease_store,
1029:         neighbor_store,
1030:         attempt_report_store,
1031:         signing_key,
1032:         agent_keys,
1033:         replay,
1034:         clock,
1035:         connection_attempt,
1036:     )
1037:     .map_err(|error| match error {
1038:         SessionHandlerError::Legacy(message) => {
1039:             classify_legacy_session_error(message)
1040:         }
1041:         SessionHandlerError::Classified(error) => error,
1042:     })
1043: }

1045: #[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
1046: fn serve_one_connection_impl(
1047:     config: &CoordinatorConfig,
1048:     stream: &mut std::net::TcpStream,
1049:     lease_store: &mut Option<CoordinatorLeaseStore>,
1050:     neighbor_store: &mut Option<crate::neighbor_report_store::CoordinatorNeighborReportStore>,
1051:     attempt_report_store: &mut Option<crate::attempt_report_store::CoordinatorAttemptReportStore>,
1052:     signing_key: &SigningKey,
1053:     agent_keys: &InMemoryKeyring,
1054:     replay: &mut InMemoryReplayGuard,
1055:     clock: &SystemClock,
1056:     connection_attempt: u32,
1057: ) -> Result<(), SessionHandlerError> {
1058:     let now = clock.now_unix_ms();
1059:     if config.resume_protocol {
1060:         return serve_resume_connection(

1062:             stream,
1063:             lease_store,
1064:             signing_key,
1065:             agent_keys,
1066:             replay,
1067:             clock,
1068:             connection_attempt,
1069:         );

1071:     // ★ D2 (B+E 구현 단계 4) — 순차 lane 도 Agent 의 Hello 로 시작한다. Grant 를 쓰기 **전에** 읽고 검증한다.

1073:     let hello = read_session_hello(config, stream, agent_keys, replay, clock)?;

1075:         return serve_renew_session(config, stream, lease_store, signing_key, agent_keys, replay, clock);
1076:     }
1077:     if hello.mode != gputeer_protocol::constants::MODE_MULTI_AGENT_GRANT {
1078:         return Err(session_protocol_error(format!(
1079:             "HELLO_REJECTED: mode 불일치 — 순차 lane 은 FRESH({}) · RENEW({}) 만 받는다, 받은 값 {}",
1080:             gputeer_protocol::constants::MODE_MULTI_AGENT_GRANT,
1081:             gputeer_protocol::constants::MODE_RENEW,

1095:         //   가 하고 이 lane 은 전송만 한다 — CLI `issue-grant` 와 **같은

1103:                 .map_err(|e| SessionHandlerError::Classified(CoordinatorSessionError::Storage(format!("lease store: {e}"))))?;
1104:             // ★ 저장된 Manifest 를 싣기 전에 **지금** 다시 검증할 제출자 keyring.

1144:             .map_err(|e| match e {

1154:     };

1156:     if config.corrupt_own_signature {

1158:             .coordinator_signature

2034: fn accept_with_deadline(
2035:     listener: &TcpListener,
2036:     timeout: Duration,
2037: ) -> Result<(std::net::TcpStream, std::net::SocketAddr), String> {
2038:     let deadline = std::time::Instant::now() + timeout;
2039:     loop {
2040:         match listener.accept() {
2041:             Ok(connection) => return Ok(connection),
2042:             Err(error) if error.kind() == ErrorKind::WouldBlock => {
2043:                 if std::time::Instant::now() >= deadline {
2044:                     return Err("accept timeout exceeded".into());
2045:                 }
2046:                 std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(5));
2047:             }
2048:             Err(error) => return Err(format!("accept failed: {error}")),
2049:         }
2050:     }
2051: }

2053: /// 받은 프레임의 **종류 이름**만 돌려준다 — 오류 문자열에 상대 메시지의 내용을 넣지 않기 위해서다(결함 92, 재검수 60).

crates/coordinator/tests/attempt_report_ingress.rs
1: //! Agent 가 보낸 `AttemptReport` 가 **wire 를 건너 저장소에 도달하는가**,
2: //! 그리고 **도달하면 안 되는 것이 안 도달하는가**.
3: //!
4: //! ★ 이 테스트는 Coordinator 의 **production 진입점**(`run()`)을 그대로
5: //!   부른다. 세션 함수는 비공개이므로, 여기서 손으로 만든 세션을 흉내 내면
6: //!   실제로 도는 코드와 다른 것을 재게 된다.
7: //!
8: //! 테스트 쪽이 Agent 역할을 한다 — Grant 를 받고, `AgentGrantAck` 를 서명해
9: //! 보내고, 그다음 `AttemptReport` 프레임을 보낸다.
10: //!
11: //! # 여기서 재지 **못하는** 것 (정직하게 적는다)
12: //!
13: //! ```text
14: //! node_id 대조          이 lane 은 Agent 키를 **하나만** 등록한다.
15: //!                       `AttemptReport::signer_id()` 가 `node_id` 라
16: //!                       다른 이름을 실으면 서명 검증이 그 이름의 키를
17: //!                       못 찾아 먼저 막는다(UnknownSigner) — 대조에
18: //!                       닿지 않는다. heartbeat·이웃 신고 경로가 같은
19: //!                       이유로 같은 공백을 갖고 있다
20: //!
21: //! Lease 부재 분기        이 lane 의 Grant 는 항상 Lease 를 싣는다.
22: //!                       그 분기는 나중에 가정이 깨졌을 때를 위한
23: //!                       fail-closed 이지 지금 도달하는 경로가 아니다
24: //!
25: //! Agent 가 실제로 보내는가  Agent 의 `run()` 은 워크로드를 **실제로 실행**
26: //!                       해야 종료를 관측한다. 그 경로는 이 테스트가
27: //!                       띄우지 않는다 — 여기서 재는 것은 수신·검증·저장
28: //!                       쪽이다. 발신 쪽은 `crates/agent/src/report.rs`
29: //!                       의 단위 테스트가 잰다
30: //! ```
31:

54: const JOB_ID: &str = "job-1";
55: const ATTEMPT_ID: &str = "attempt-1";
56: const LEASE_ID: &str = "lease-1";
57: /// Agent 의 장치 ID 이자 예약된 노드 이름. `AttemptReport.node_id` 이며
58: /// 동시에 `signer_id` 다 — 셋이 같아야 저장소가 결합한다.
59: const NODE_ID: &str = "node-1";
60: const COORDINATOR_ID: &str = "coordinator-1";
61: const GRANT_ID: &str = "grant-1";
62: 
63: const COORDINATOR_SEED: [u8; 32] = [0x31; 32];
64: const AGENT_SEED: [u8; 32] = [0x41; 32];
65: const SUBMITTER_ID: &str = "submitter-1";
66: const SUBMITTER_SEED: [u8; 32] = [0x51; 32];
67: 
68: fn now_ms() -> u64 {
69:     SystemTime::now()
70:         .duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH)
71:         .expect("시계가 UNIX epoch 뒤에 있다")
72:         .as_millis() as u64
73: }
74: 
75: fn hex(bytes: &[u8]) -> String {
76:     bytes.iter().map(|b| format!("{b:02x}")).collect()
77: }
78: 
79: /// Attempt·Lease·노드 예약이 durable 하게 있는 control DB.
80: ///
81: /// `tests/reservation_release.rs` 의 fixture 와 같은 모양이다 — 다만 Lease
82: /// 시각을 **지금 기준**으로 둔다. `signed_grant_from_stored()` 가 발급
83: /// 시각에 Lease 가 살아 있는지 보기 때문이다.
84: fn prepare_control_db(path: &Path) {
85:     let now = now_ms();
86: 
87:     let mut inventory = CoordinatorInventoryStore::open(path).expect("inventory store");
88:     inventory
89:         .register_agent(&AgentRegistry {
90:             node_id: NODE_ID.into(),
91:             device_id: NODE_ID.into(),
92:             owner_member_id: "owner-1".into(),
93:             verifying_key: SigningKey::from_bytes(&AGENT_SEED)
94:                 .verifying_key()
95:                 .to_bytes()
96:                 .to_vec(),
97:             node_state: None,
98:             risk_state: None,
99:             security_tier: None,
100:             isolation_class: None,
101:             key_protection: None,
102:         })
103:         .expect("register agent");
104:     inventory
105:         .update_inventory(&AgentInventory {
106:             node_id: NODE_ID.into(),
107:             inventory_revision: 7,
108:             observed_at_unix_ms: now,
109:             gpus: Some(vec![GpuInventory {
110:                 gpu_id: "gpu-1".into(),
111:                 model: Some("model-1".into()),
112:                 healthy: Some(true),
113:                 available_vram_bytes: Some(16),
114:             }]),
115:             available_cpu_cores: Some(8),
116:             available_ram_bytes: Some(64),
117:             available_workspace_bytes: Some(64),
118:             allowed_workload_classes: None,
119:             third_party_workloads_opt_in: None,
120:         })
121:         .expect("update inventory");
122:     drop(inventory);
123: 
124:     // ★ 저장된 예약 lane 은 이제 제출자 서명 Manifest 를 **지금** 다시 검증해
125:     //   싣는다(§A1 1.5 선행). 그래서 hash 만 있는 Job 이 아니라 서명된 Manifest 를
126:     //   묶어 저장한다 — 없으면 `LegacyManifestMissing` 으로 발급이 거부된다.
127:     let submitter = SigningKey::from_bytes(&SUBMITTER_SEED);
128:     let mut manifest = pb::JobManifest {
129:         schema_version: 1,
130:         job_id: JOB_ID.into(),
131:         entrypoint: "train.py".into(),
132:         submitter_device_id: SUBMITTER_ID.into(),
133:         issued_at_unix_ms: now.saturating_sub(60_000),
134:         expires_at_unix_ms: now + 7 * 24 * 3_600_000,
135:         ..Default::default()
136:     };
137:     manifest.submitter_signature = sign(&submitter, &manifest).to_vec();
138:     let mut ring = InMemoryKeyring::new();
139:     ring.insert(SUBMITTER_ID, submitter.verifying_key());
140:     let verified = verify(&manifest, 1, &Ed25519Verifier::new(ring), now, &mut NoReplayCheck)
141:         .expect("fixture Manifest 서명이 검증된다");
142:     let mut jobs = CoordinatorJobStore::open(path).expect("job store");
143:     jobs.submit_verified_manifest(
144:         &AcceptedJobSubmission {
145:             idempotency_key: [1; 16],
146:             job_id: JOB_ID.into(),
147:             submitter_device_id: SUBMITTER_ID.into(),
148:             manifest_hash: blake3_256(&signing_input(&manifest)),
149:             deadline_unix_ms: Some(now + 3_600_000),
150:             max_queue_duration_ms: Some(3_600_000),
151:         },
152:         &verified,
153:         now,
154:     )
155:     .expect("submit");
156:     jobs.start_planning(JOB_ID, now).expect("planning");
157:     jobs.enqueue(JOB_ID, "plan-1", now).expect("enqueue");
158:     drop(jobs);
159: 
160:     CoordinatorStagingStore::open(path)
161:         .expect("staging store")
162:         .reserve_node_and_stage_queued_with_lease(
163:             &StageQueuedRequest {
164:                 operation_key: [2; 16],
165:                 job_id: JOB_ID.into(),
166:                 attempt_id: ATTEMPT_ID.into(),
167:                 lease_id: LEASE_ID.into(),
168:                 node_id: NODE_ID.into(),
169:                 selected_gpu_ids: vec!["gpu-1".into()],
170:                 issuing_coordinator_id: COORDINATOR_ID.into(),
171:                 coordinator_term: 1,
172:                 issued_at_unix_ms: now,
173:                 renew_after_unix_ms: now + 300_000,
174:                 expires_at_unix_ms: now + 600_000,
175:                 max_total_duration_seconds: 3_600,
176:             },
177:             7,
178:         )
179:         .expect("reserve + stage");
180: }
181: 
182: fn staged_fence_epoch(path: &Path) -> u64 {
183:     CoordinatorStagingStore::open(path)
184:         .expect("staging store")
185:         .get_attempt(ATTEMPT_ID)
186:         .expect("attempt 조회")
187:         .expect("fixture 가 Attempt 를 만들었다")
188:         .fence_epoch
189: }

191: /// 커널이 고른 포트를 쓰되, Coordinator 가 그 주소로 bind 하도록
192: /// **먼저 잡았다가 놓는다.** `run()` 은 실제 주소를 stdout 에만 찍어서
193: /// 라이브러리 호출자가 읽을 방법이 없다.
194: fn reserve_loopback_port() -> SocketAddr {
195:     let reserved = TcpListener::bind("127.0.0.1:0").expect("포트 예약");
196:     let address = reserved.local_addr().expect("주소");
197:     drop(reserved);
198:     address
199: }

222:     Fixture {

227:     }
